/(?:请记住|记住)[:：]?\\s*(.+)/,\n    /帮我记住[:：]?\\s*(.+)/,\n    /以后都(?:要|用|按|记住)\\s*(.+)/,\n    /我(?:的)?偏好(?:是|：|:)\\s*(.+)/,\n    /从现在起\\s*(.+)/,\n    /务必记住\\s*(.+)/,\n  ]\n  for (const pattern of patterns) {\n    const match = text.match(pattern)\n    const content = match?.[1]?.trim()\n    if (content && content.length >= 4) return content\n  }\n}\n\nfunction memoryScope(input: {\n  projectID?: AddInput[\"projectID\"]\n  userContent?: string\n  content?: string\n  scope?: AddInput[\"scope\"]\n}) {\n  return resolveMemoryScope({\n    projectID: input.projectID,\n    userContent: input.userContent,\n    content: input.content,\n    scope: input.scope as MemoryScopeName | undefined,\n  })\n}\n\nfunction memoryProjectID(projectID: AddInput[\"projectID\"] | undefined, scope: AddInput[\"scope\"]) {\n  return scopeProjectID(projectID, scope as MemoryScopeName)\n}\n\nfunction clampUnit(input: number | undefined, fallback: number) {\n  if (typeof input !== \"number\" || !Number.isFinite(input)) return fallback\n  return Math.min(Math.max(input, 0), 1)\n}\n\nfunction candidateRow(\n  input: ReviewInput,\n  proposal: ReviewCandidateProposal,\n): typeof MemoryReviewCandidateTable.$inferInsert | undefined {\n  const content = proposal.content.trim()\n  if (content.length < 4) return\n  const now = Date.now()\n  const scope = memoryScope({\n    projectID: input.projectID,\n    userContent: input.userContent,\n    content,\n    scope: proposal.scope,\n  })\n  const tags = Array.from(new Set([...(proposal.tags ?? []), \"review\"].map((tag) => tag.trim()).filter(Boolean)))\n  const domain = resolveMemoryDomain({\n    domain: proposal.domain as MemoryDomain | undefined,\n    content,\n    userContent: input.userContent,\n    tags,\n  })\n  return {\n    id: ReviewCandidateID.ascending(),\n    project_id: memoryProjectID(input.projectID, scope),\n    session_id: input.sessionID,\n    target: proposal.target ?? \"memory\",\n    scope,\n    domain,\n    content,\n    summary: proposal.summary?.trim() || (content.length > 120 ? `${content.slice(0, 117)}...` : undefined),\n    tags,\n    importance: clampUnit(proposal.importance, 0.7),\n    confidence: clampUnit(proposal.confidence, 0.7),\n    fact_key: deriveFactKey(content, proposal.factKey),\n    operation: resolveMemoryOperation(proposal.operation),\n    reason: proposal.reason?.trim() || \"LLM 归纳出可能值得长期保留的信息\",\n    source_message_id: input.sourceMessageID,\n    status: \"pending\",\n    time_created: now,\n    time_updated: now,\n  }\n}\n\nfunction candidateRowsFromReview(input: ReviewInput, tags: string[] = []) {\n  const explicit = explicitMemory(input.userContent)\n  return [\n    explicit\n      ? {\n          content: explicit,\n          tags: [\"explicit\", ...tags],\n          importance: 0.8,\n          confidence: 0.9,\n          operation: \"add\" as const,\n          reason: \"用户显式要求记住该内容\",\n        }\n      : undefined,\n    ...(input.candidates ?? []).map((candidate) => ({\n      ...candidate,\n      tags: [...(candidate.tags ?? []), ...tags],\n    })),\n  ]\n    .filter((item) => item !== undefined)\n    .map((proposal) => candidateRow(input, proposal))\n    .filter((item) => item !== undefined)\n    .filter((row, index, rows) => rows.findIndex((item) => item.content === row.content) === index)\n}\n\nfunction reviewInterval(input?: number) {\n  if (typeof input !== \"number\" || !Number.isFinite(input)) return 1\n  return Math.max(Math.floor(input), 1)\n}\n\nfunction targetSegments(target: string) {\n  return target\n    .trim()\n    .replace(/\\.md$/i, \"\")\n    .split(/[\\\\/]+/)\n    .map((item) => item.trim().replace(/[<>:\"|?*\\x00-\\x1f]/g, \"-\"))\n    .filter((item) => item && item !== \".\" && item !== \"..\")\n}\n\nfunction memoryFile(row: typeof MemoryEntryTable.$inferInsert, location: MemoryFileLocation) {\n  const root = path.join(location.worktree === \"/\" ? location.directory : location.worktree, \".novaway\", \"memory\")\n  const segments = targetSegments(row.target)\n  const file = path.join(root, row.scope, ...(segments.length ? segments : [\"memory\"]), `${row.id}.md`)\n  if (!AppFileSystem.contains(root, file)) return path.join(root, \"project\", \"memory\", `${row.id}.md`)\n  return file\n}\n\nfunction memoryMarkdown(row: typeof MemoryEntryTable.$inferInsert) {\n  const title = row.summary || row.content.slice(0, 48)\n  const tags = row.tags ?? []\n  const timeCreated = typeof row.time_created === \"number\" ? row.time_created : Date.now()\n  return [\n    `# ${title}`,\n    \"\",\n    `- 记忆ID: ${row.id}`,\n    `- 范围: ${row.scope}`,\n    `- 领域: ${domainLabel((row.domain ?? \"general\") as MemoryDomain)}`,\n    `- 目标: ${row.target}`,\n    `- 来源: ${row.source}`,\n    `- 重要性: ${row.importance}`,\n    `- 置信度: ${row.confidence ?? 0.7}`,\n    `- 事实键: ${row.fact_key || \"无\"}`,\n    `- 版本: ${row.version ?? 1}`,\n    `- 标签: ${tags.join(\", \") || \"无\"}`,\n    `- 创建时间: ${new Date(timeCreated).toISOString()}`,\n    \"\",\n    \"## 内容\",\n    \"\",\n    row.content,\n    \"\",\n  ].join(\"\\n\")\n}\n\nasync function writeMemoryFile(row: typeof MemoryEntryTable.$inferInsert, location: MemoryFileLocation) {\n  const file = memoryFile(row, location)\n  await mkdir(path.dirname(file), { recursive: true })\n  await writeFile(file, memoryMarkdown(row))\n  return file\n}\n\nfunction searchFtsIds(query: string, limit: number) {\n  const match = sanitizeFtsQuery(query)\n  if (!match) return [] as string[]\n  try {\n    return Database.use((db) => {\n      const client = db.$client as {\n        query: (sql: string) => { all: (...args: unknown[]) => Array<{ id: string }> }\n      }\n      // FTS5 在部分绑定器下对 `?` 语法不友好，这里使用转义后的字面量查询\n      const safe = match.replace(/'/g, \"''\")\n      return client\n        .query(\n          `select id from memory_entry_fts where memory_entry_fts match '${safe}' order by bm25(memory_entry_fts) limit ${Math.max(1, Math.min(limit, 200))}`,\n        )\n        .all()\n        .map((row) => row.id)\n    })\n  } catch {\n    return [] as string[]\n  }\n}\n\nexport const layer: Layer.Layer<Service, never, Bus.Service> = Layer.effect(\n  Service,\n  Effect.gen(function* () {\n    const bus = yield* Bus.Service\n    const publishReviewUpdated = (input: { projectID?: AddInput[\"projectID\"]; sessionID?: AddInput[\"sessionID\"] }) =>\n      bus\n        .publish(Event.ReviewUpdated, {\n          projectID: input.projectID,\n          sessionID: input.sessionID,\n        })\n        .pipe(Effect.catchCause(() => Effect.void))\n\n    const list: Interface[\"list\"] = Effect.fn(\"Memory.list\")(function* (input) {\n      const rows = yield* Effect.sync(() =>\n        Database.use((db) =>\n          db\n            .select()\n            .from(MemoryEntryTable)\n            .where(and(...conditions(input)))\n            .orderBy(desc(MemoryEntryTable.time_updated))\n            .limit(input?.limit ?? DEFAULT_LIMIT)\n            .all(),\n        ),\n      )\n      const items = rows.map(rowToInfo)\n      const search = input?.search?.trim()\n      if (!search) return items\n\n      const ftsIds = searchFtsIds(search, input?.limit ?? DEFAULT_LIMIT)\n      if (!ftsIds.length) {\n        return items\n          .map((item) => ({ item, score: scoreMemory(search, item) }))\n          .filter((item) => item.score >= 10)\n          .toSorted((a, b) => b.score - a.score)\n          .map((item) => item.item)\n      }\n\n      const extra = ftsIds.length\n        ? yield* Effect.sync(() =>\n            Database.use((db) =>\n              db\n                .select()\n                .from(MemoryEntryTable)\n                .where(and(...conditions(input), sql`${MemoryEntryTable.id} in ${ftsIds}`))\n                .all(),\n            ),\n          )\n        : []\n      const byId = new Map([...items, ...extra.map(rowToInfo)].map((item) => [item.id, item]))\n      return mergeHybridCandidates({\n        query: search,\n        keywordItems: items,\n        ftsIds,\n        byId,\n        limit: input?.limit ?? DEFAULT_LIMIT,\n      })\n    })\n\n    const getByID = Effect.fn(\"Memory.getByID\")(function* (id: MemoryID) {\n      const row = yield* Effect.sync(() =>\n        Database.use((db) => db.select().from(MemoryEntryTable).where(eq(MemoryEntryTable.id, id)).get()),\n      )\n      return row ? rowToInfo(row) : undefined\n    })\n\n    const findActiveByFactKey = Effect.fn(\"Memory.findActiveByFactKey\")(function* (input: {\n      factKey?: string\n      projectID?: AddInput[\"projectID\"]\n      scope: AddInput[\"scope\"]\n      target: AddInput[\"target\"]\n    }) {\n      if (!input.factKey) return undefined as Info | undefined\n      const rows = yield* Effect.sync(() =>\n        Database.use((db) =>\n          db\n            .select()\n            .from(MemoryEntryTable)\n            .where(\n              and(\n                isNull(MemoryEntryTable.time_archived),\n                eq(MemoryEntryTable.fact_key, input.factKey!),\n                eq(MemoryEntryTable.scope, input.scope ?? \"project\"),\n                eq(MemoryEntryTable.target, input.target ?? \"memory\"),\n                input.scope === \"global\"\n                  ? isNull(MemoryEntryTable.project_id)\n                  : input.projectID\n                    ? eq(MemoryEntryTable.project_id, input.projectID)\n                    : undefined,\n              ),\n            )\n            .orderBy(desc(MemoryEntryTable.version), desc(MemoryEntryTable.time_updated))\n            .limit(1)\n            .all(),\n        ),\n      )\n      return rows[0] ? rowToInfo(rows[0]) : undefined\n    })\n\n    const archiveByID = Effect.fn(\"Memory.archiveByID\")(function* (id: MemoryID, validTo?: number) {\n      const now = Date.now()\n      yield* Effect.sync(() =>\n        Database.use((db) =>\n          db\n            .update(MemoryEntryTable)\n            .set({\n              time_archived: now,\n              valid_to: validTo ?? now,\n              time_updated: now,\n            })\n            .where(eq(MemoryEntryTable.id, id))\n            .run(),\n        ),\n      )\n    })\n\n    const add: Interface[\"add\"] = Effect.fn(\"Memory.add\")(function* (input) {\n      const now = Date.now()\n      const scope = memoryScope({\n        projectID: input.projectID,\n        content: input.content,\n        userContent: input.content,\n        scope: input.scope,\n      })\n      const tags = input.tags ? Array.from(input.tags) : []\n      const domain = resolveMemoryDomain({\n        domain: input.domain as MemoryDomain | undefined,\n        content: input.content,\n        tags,\n      })\n      const factKey = deriveFactKey(input.content, input.factKey)\n      const operation = resolveMemoryOperation(input.operation)\n      const existing = yield* findActiveByFactKey({\n        factKey,\n        projectID: memoryProjectID(input.projectID, scope),\n        scope,\n        target: input.target ?? \"memory\",\n      })\n\n      if (existing && operation === \"confirm\") {\n        return (yield* update({\n          id: existing.id,\n          confidence: Math.max(existing.confidence, clampUnit(input.confidence, existing.confidence)),\n          confirm: true,\n        }))!\n      }\n\n      if (existing && operation === \"archive\") {\n        yield* archiveByID(existing.id, input.validTo ?? \"manual\",\n          origin_message_id: input.originMessageID,\n          created_by: input.createdBy,\n          time_created: now,\n          time_updated: now,\n          valid_from: input.validFrom ?? now,\n          valid_to: input.validTo,\n          last_confirmed_at: now,\n        }\n        if (input.location) yield* Effect.promise(() => writeMemoryFile(row, input.location!))\n        yield* Effect.sync(() => Database.use((db) => db.insert(MemoryEntryTable).values(row).run()))\n        return (yield* getByID(row.id))!\n      }\n\n      const row: typeof MemoryEntryTable.$inferInsert = {\n        id: MemoryID.ascending(),\n        project_id: memoryProjectID(input.projectID, scope),\n        session_id: input.sessionID,\n        target: input.target ?? \"memory\",\n        scope,\n        domain,\n        content: input.content.trim(),\n        summary: input.summary?.trim() || undefined,\n        tags,\n        importance: input.importance ?? 0.5,\n        confidence: clampUnit(input.confidence, 0.7),\n        fact_key: factKey,\n        version: 1,\n        source: input.source ?? \"manual\",\n        origin_message_id: input.originMessageID,\n        created_by: input.createdBy,\n        time_created: now,\n        time_updated: now,\n        valid_from: input.validFrom ?? now,\n        valid_to: input.validTo,\n        last_confirmed_at: now,\n      }\n      if (input.location) yield* Effect.promise(() => writeMemoryFile(row, input.location!))\n      yield* Effect.sync(() => Database.use((db) => db.insert(MemoryEntryTable).values(row).run()))\n      return (yield* getByID(row.id))!\n    })\n\n    const update: Interface[\"update\"] = Effect.fn(\"Memory.update\")(function* (input) {\n      const now = Date.now()\n      const existing = yield* getByID(input.id)\n      if (!existing) return\n      const nextScope =\n        input.scope === undefined\n          ? existing.tags,\n        })\n      yield* Effect.sync(() =>\n        Database.use((db) =>\n          db\n            .update(MemoryEntryTable)\n            .set({\n              ...(input.content === undefined ? now : null }),\n              ...(input.validFrom === undefined ? {} : { valid_from: input.validFrom }),\n              ...(input.validTo === undefined ? {} : { valid_to: input.validTo }),\n              ...(input.confirm ? { last_confirmed_at: now, confidence: Math.min(1, existing.confidence + 0.05) } : {}),\n              time_updated: now,\n            })\n            .where(eq(MemoryEntryTable.id, input.id))\n            .run(),\n        ),\n      )\n      return yield* getByID(input.id)\n    })\n\n    const remove: Interface[\"remove\"] = Effect.fn(\"Memory.remove\")(function* (id) {\n      const existing = yield* getByID(id)\n      if (!existing) return false\n      yield* Effect.sync(() =>\n        Database.use((db) => db.delete(MemoryEntryTable).where(eq(MemoryEntryTable.id, id)).run()),\n      )\n      return true\n    })\n\n    const prefetch: Interface[\"prefetch\"] = Effect.fn(\"Memory.prefetch\")(function* (input) {\n      if (!shouldPrefetch(input.query)) return \"\"\n      const pool = yield* list({\n        projectID: input.projectID,\n        includeGlobal: true,\n        search: input.query,\n        limit: 200,\n      })\n      return buildPrefetchText(input.query, pool, {\n        limit: input.limit ?? DEFAULT_PREFETCH_LIMIT,\n        maxChars: input.maxChars ?? DEFAULT_PREFETCH_BUDGET_CHARS,\n      })\n    })\n\n    const syncTurn: Interface[\"syncTurn\"] = Effect.fn(\"Memory.syncTurn\")(function* (input) {\n      const content = explicitMemory(input.userContent)\n      if (!content) return\n      yield* add({\n        projectID: input.projectID,\n        sessionID: input.sessionID,\n        target: \"memory\",\n        scope: memoryScope({ projectID: input.projectID, userContent: input.userContent }),\n        content,\n        source: \"turn\",\n        originMessageID: input.originMessageID,\n        createdBy: input.agent,\n        tags: [\"explicit\"],\n        importance: 0.8,\n        confidence: 0.9,\n        operation: \"add\",\n      })\n    })\n\n    const status: Interface[\"status\"] = Effect.fn(\"Memory.status\")(function* (input) {\n      const items = yield* list({\n        projectID: input?.projectID,\n        sessionID: input?.sessionID,\n        includeArchived: true,\n        includeExpired: true,\n        limit: 10_000,\n      })\n      return {\n        total: items.length,\n        active: items.filter((item) => item.time.archived === undefined).length,\n        archived: items.filter((item) => item.time.archived !== undefined).length,\n        latest: items[0]?.time.updated,\n      }\n    })\n\n    const listReviewCandidates: Interface[\"listReviewCandidates\"] = Effect.fn(\"Memory.listReviewCandidates\")(\n      function* (input) {\n        const rows = yield* Effect.sync(() =>\n          Database.use((db) =>\n            db\n              .select()\n              .from(MemoryReviewCandidateTable)\n              .where(and(...reviewConditions(input)))\n              .orderBy(desc(MemoryReviewCandidateTable.time_updated))\n              .limit(input?.limit ?? last_reviewed_message_id: input.sourceMessageID,\n              last_reviewed_at: now,\n              time_created: current?.time_created ?? now,\n              time_updated: now,\n            })\n return next\n        }),\n      )\n      return turn % interval === 0\n    })\n\n    const reviewDue: Interface[\"reviewDue\"] = Effect.fn(\"Memory.reviewDue\")(function* (input) {\n      return === input.sourceMessageID && item.content === row.content),\n        )\n        .filter((item) => item !== undefined)\n\n      if (pending.length) {\n        yield* Effect.sync(() => Database.use((db) return yield* persistCandidates(input, rows)\n    })\n\n    const reviewCompaction: Interface[\"reviewCompaction\"] = Effect.fn(\"Memory.reviewCompaction\")(function* (input) {\n      const text =     userContent: text,\n          projectID: input.projectID,\n          sessionID: input.sessionID,\n          sourceMessageID: input.sourceMessageID,\n        },\n        rows,\n      )\n    })\n\n   all: counts.all + 1,\n      [source]: counts[source] + 1,\n    }\n  }, emptyReviewSourceCounts())\n}\n\nfunction explicitMemory(text: string) {\n  const patterns = [\n    /(?:please\\s+)?remember(?:\\s+that)?\\s+(.+)/i,\n    /(?:请记住|记住)[:：]?\\s*(.+)/,\n    /帮我记住[:：]?\\s*(.+)/,\n    /以后都(?:要|用|按|记住)\\s*(.+)/,\n    /我(?:的)?偏好(?:是|：|:)\\s*(.+)/,\n    /从现在起\\s*(.+)/,\n }\n}\n\nfunction memoryScope(input: {\n  projectID?: AddInput[\"projectID\"]\n  userContent?: string\n  content?: string\n  scope?: AddInput[\"scope\"]\n}) {\n  return resolveMemoryScope({\n {\n  if (typeof input !== \"number\" || !Number.isFinite(input)) return fallback\n  return Math.min(Math.max(input, 0), 1)\n}\n'''\npath.write_text(path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\") + text, {

[]), \"review\"].map((tag) => tag.trim()).filter(Boolean)))\n  const domain = resolveMemoryDomain({\n    domain: proposal.domain as MemoryDomain | undefined,\n    content,\n    userContent: input.userContent,\n    tags,\n  })\n  return {\n    id: ReviewCandidateID.ascending(),\n    project_id: memoryProjectID(input.projectID, scope),\n    session_id: input.sessionID,\n    target: proposal.target ?? \"memory\",\n    scope,\n    domain,\n    content,\n    summary: proposal.summary?.trim() || (content.length > 120 ? `${content.slice(0, 117)}...` : undefined),\n    tags,\n    importance: clampUnit(proposal.importance, 0.7),\n    confidence: clampUnit(proposal.confidence, 0.7),\n    fact_key: deriveFactKey(content, proposal.factKey),\n    operation: resolveMemoryOperation(proposal.operation),\n    reason: proposal.reason?.trim() || \"LLM 归纳出可能值得长期保留的信息\",\n    source_message_id: input.sourceMessageID,\n    status: \"pending\",\n    time_created: now,\n    time_updated: now,\n  }\n}\n\nfunction candidateRowsFromReview(input: ReviewInput, tags: string[] = []) {\n  const explicit = explicitMemory(input.userContent)\n  return [\n    explicit\n      ? {\n          content: explicit,\n          tags: [\"explicit\", ...tags],\n          importance: 0.8,\n          confidence: 0.9,\n          operation: \"add\" as const,\n          reason: \"用户显式要求记住该内容\",\n        }\n      : undefined,\n    ...(input.candidates ?? []).map((candidate) => ({\n      ...candidate,\n      tags: [...(candidate.tags ?? []), ...tags],\n    })),\n  ]\n    .filter((item) => item !== undefined)\n    .map((proposal) => candidateRow(input, proposal))\n    .filter((item) => item !== undefined)\n    .filter((row, index, rows) => rows.findIndex((item) => item.content === row.content) === index)\n}\n\nfunction reviewInterval(input?: number) {\n  if (typeof input !== \"number\" || !Number.isFinite(input)) return 1\n  return Math.max(Math.floor(input), 1)\n}\n\nfunction targetSegments(target: string) {\n  return target\n    .trim()\n    .replace(/\\.md$/i, \"\")\n    .split(/[\\\\/]+/)\n    .map((item) => item.trim().replace(/[<>:\"|?*\\x00-\\x1f]/g, \"-\"))\n    .filter((item) => item && item !== \".\" && item !== \"..\")\n}\n\nfunction memoryFile(row: typeof MemoryEntryTable.$inferInsert, location: MemoryFileLocation) {\n  const root = path.join(location.worktree === \"/\" ? location.directory : location.worktree, \".novaway\", \"memory\")\n  const segments = targetSegments(row.target)\n  const file = path.join(root, row.scope, ...(segments.length ? segments : [\"memory\"]), `${row.id}.md`)\n  if (!AppFileSystem.contains(root, file)) return path.join(root, \"project\", \"memory\", `${row.id}.md`)\n  return file\n}\n\nfunction memoryMarkdown(row: typeof MemoryEntryTable.$inferInsert) {\n  const title = row.summary || row.content.slice(0, 48)\n  const tags = row.tags ?? []\n  const timeCreated = typeof row.time_created === \"number\" ? row.time_created : Date.now()\n  return [\n    `# ${title}`,\n    \"\",\n    `- 记忆ID: ${row.id}`,\n    `- 范围: ${row.scope}`,\n    `- 领域: ${domainLabel((row.domain ?? \"general\") as MemoryDomain)}`,\n    `- 目标: ${row.target}`,\n    `- 来源: ${row.source}`,\n    `- 重要性: ${row.importance}`,\n    `- 置信度: ${row.confidence ?? 0.7}`,\n    `- 事实键: ${row.fact_key || \"无\"}`,\n    `- 版本: ${row.version ?? 1}`,\n    `- 标签: ${tags.join(\", \") || \"无\"}`,\n    `- 创建时间: ${new Date(timeCreated).toISOString()}`,\n    \"\",\n    \"## 内容\",\n    \"\",\n    row.content,\n    \"\",\n  ].join(\"\\n\")\n}\n\nasync function writeMemoryFile(row: typeof MemoryEntryTable.$inferInsert, location: MemoryFileLocation) {\n  const file = memoryFile(row, location)\n  await mkdir(path.dirname(file), { recursive: true })\n  await writeFile(file, memoryMarkdown(row))\n  return file\n}\n\nfunction searchFtsIds(query: string, limit: number) {\n  const match = sanitizeFtsQuery(query)\n  if (!match) return [] as string[]\n  try {\n    return Database.use((db) => {\n      const client = db.$client as {\n        query: (sqlText: string) => { all: (...args: unknown[]) => Array<{ id: string }> }\n      }\n      const safe = match.replace(/'/g, \"''\")\n      return client\n        .query(\n          `select id from memory_entry_fts where memory_entry_fts match '${safe}' order by bm25(memory_entry_fts) limit ${Math.max(1, Math.min(limit, 200))}`,\n        )\n        .all()\n        .map((row) => row.id)\n    })\n  } catch {\n    return [] as string[]\n  }\n}\n'''\npath.write_text(path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\") + text, encoding=\"utf-8\")\nprint(\"part3\", path.stat().st_size)\n'@ | python -",

}

DEFAULT_LIMIT)\n            .all(),\n        ),\n      )\n      const items = rows.map(rowToInfo)\n      const search = input?.search?.trim()\n      if (!search) return items\n\n      const ftsIds = searchFtsIds(search, input?.limit ?? rowToInfo(row) : undefined\n    })\n\n    const findActiveByFactKey = Effect.fn(\"Memory.findActiveByFactKey\")(function* (input: {\n      factKey?: string\n      projectID?: AddInput[\"projectID\"]\n      scope: AddInput[\"scope\"]\n      target: AddInput[\"target\"]\n    }) {\n      if (!input.factKey) return undefined as Info | undefined\n      const rows = yield* Effect.sync(() =>\n        Database.use((db) =>\n          db\n            .select()\n            .from(MemoryEntryTable)\n            .where(\n              and(\n                isNull(MemoryEntryTable.time_archived),\n                eq(MemoryEntryTable.fact_key, input.factKey!),\n                eq(MemoryEntryTable.scope, input.scope ?? eq(MemoryEntryTable.project_id, input.projectID)\n                    : undefined,\n              ),\n            )\n            .orderBy(desc(MemoryEntryTable.version), desc(MemoryEntryTable.time_updated))\n            .limit(1)\n            .all(),\n        ),\n      )\n      return rows[0] ? now,\n              time_updated: now,\n            })\n            .where(eq(MemoryEntryTable.id, id))\n            .run(),\n        ),\n      )\n    })\n'''\npath.write_text(path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\") + text, encoding=\"utf-8\")\nprint(\"part4\", path.stat().st_size)\n'@ | python -",

Global.Path.config } : input,\n        ),\n      )\n      const now = Date.now()\n      yield* Effect.sync(() =>\n        Database.use((db) =>\n          db\n            .update(EvolutionCandidateTable)\n            .set({ status: \"applied\", time_applied: now, time_updated: now })\n            .where(eq(EvolutionCandidateTable.id, id))\n            .run(),\n        ),\n      )\n      const applied = yield* getByID(id)\n      if (!applied) return\n      yield* publishUpdated({\n        projectID: candidate.projectID,\n        sessionID: candidate.sessionID,\n      })\n      return { candidate: applied, dryRun }\n    })\n'''\nnew = '''    const applyToDisk: Interface[\"applyToDisk\"] = Effect.fn(\"Evolution.applyToDisk\")(function* (id, input) {\n      const candidate = yield* getByID(id)\n      if (!candidate || candidate.status !== \"pending\") return\n      const dryRunResult = yield* Effect.promise(() => buildDryRun(candidate, input))\n      try {\n        yield* Effect.promise(() =>\n          writeDryRun(\n            dryRunResult,\n            isGlobalCandidate(candidate) ? { ...input, worktree: input.globalConfig ?? Global.Path.config } : input,\n          ),\n        )\n      } catch (error) {\n        const now = Date.now()\n        const note = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)\n        yield* Effect.sync(() =>\n          Database.use((db) =>\n            db\n              .update(EvolutionCandidateTable)\n              .set({\n                validation_status: \"failed\",\n                validation_note: note,\n                time_updated: now,\n              })\n              .where(eq(EvolutionCandidateTable.id, id))\n              .run(),\n          ),\n        )\n        yield* publishUpdated({\n          projectID: candidate.projectID,\n          sessionID: candidate.sessionID,\n        })\n        throw error\n      }\n      const now = Date.now()\n      const note =\n        candidate.kind === \"skill\"\n          ? \"已写入 skill 文件；下一会话/重新加载后生效。\"\n          : candidate.kind === \"agent\"\n            ? \"已写入 agent 配置文件；重新加载后生效。\"\n            : \"已写入进化产物文件。\"\n      yield* Effect.sync(() =>\n        Database.use((db) =>\n          db\n            .update(EvolutionCandidateTable)\n            .set({\n              status: \"applied\",\n              validation_status: \"validated\",\n              validation_note: note,\n              time_applied: now,\n              time_updated: now,\n            })\n            .where(eq(EvolutionCandidateTable.id, id))\n            .run(),\n        ),\n      )\n      const applied = yield* getByID(id)\n      if (!applied) return\n      yield* publishUpdated({\n        projectID: candidate.projectID,\n        sessionID: candidate.sessionID,\n      })\n      return { candidate: applied, dryRun: dryRunResult }\n    })\n'''\nif old not in text:\n    raise SystemExit('applyToDisk not found')\ntext = text.replace(old, new)\npath.write_text(text, encoding=\"utf-8\")\nprint('evolution service patched')\n'@ | python -",
  "workdir": "E:\\AImoney\\NovaWay-Matrix\\novaway-coder"
}

p.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\nif \"DomainFilter\" not in t:\n    t = t.replace(\n'''export type CandidateStatus = \"pending\" | \"applied\" | \"dismissed\"\nexport type CandidateSource = \"all\" | \"background\" | \"session-end\"\nexport type ValidationStatus = \"pending\" | \"validated\" | \"failed\"\n''',\n'''export type CandidateStatus = \"pending\" | \"applied\" | \"dismissed\"\nexport type CandidateSource = \"all\" | \"background\" | \"session-end\"\nexport type ValidationStatus = \"pending\" | \"validated\" | \"failed\"\nexport type EvolutionDomain = \"general\" | \"coding\" | \"office\" | \"personal\" | \"research\" | \"ops\"\nexport type DomainFilter = \"all\" | EvolutionDomain\n\nexport const evolutionDomainFilters: DomainFilter[] = [\n  \"all\",\n  \"general\",\n  \"coding\",\n  \"office\",\n  \"personal\",\n  \"research\",\n  \"ops\",\n]\n'''\n    )\n    t = t.replace(\n'''export function evolutionValidationLabel(status: string | undefined | null) {\n  switch (status) {\n    case \"validated\":\n      return \"已验证\"\n    case \"failed\":\n      return \"验证失败\"\n    default:\n      return \"待验证\"\n  }\n}\n''',\n'''export function evolutionValidationLabel(status: string | undefined | null) {\n  switch (status) {\n    case \"validated\":\n      return \"已验证\"\n    case \"failed\":\n      return \"验证失败\"\n    default:\n      return \"待验证\"\n  }\n}\n\nexport function filterEvolutionByDomain<T extends { domain?: string | null }>(\n  items: readonly T[] | undefined | null,\n  domain: DomainFilter,\n) {\n  const list = Array.isArray(items) ? items : []\n  if (domain === \"all\") return list.slice()\n  return list.filter((item) => (item.domain ?? \"general\") === domain)\n}\n\nexport function evolutionDomainCounts(items: readonly { domain?: string | null }[] | undefined | null) {\n  const list = Array.isArray(items) ? items : []\n  const counts: Record<DomainFilter, number> = {\n    all: list.length,\n    general: 0,\n    coding: 0,\n    office: 0,\n    personal: 0,\n    research: 0,\n    ops: 0,\n  }\n  for (const item of list) {\n    const domain = (item.domain ?? \"general\") as EvolutionDomain\n    if (domain in counts) counts[domain] += 1\n  }\n  return counts\n}\n\nexport function evolutionDomainFilterLabel(domain: DomainFilter) {\n  if (domain === \"all\") return \"全部领域\"\n  return evolutionDomainLabel(domain)\n}\n'''\n    )\n    p.write_text(t, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    print(\"evolution helpers domain filter added\")\n'@ | python -",

Math.max(input?.limit ?? DEFAULT_LIMIT, 200) : (input?.limit ?? DEFAULT_LIMIT)\n      const rows = yield* Effect.sync(() =>\n        Database.use((db) =>\n          db\n            .select()\n            .from(MemoryEntryTable)\n            .where(and(...conditions(input)))\n            .orderBy(desc(MemoryEntryTable.time_updated))\n            .limit(poolLimit)\n            .all(),\n        ),\n      )\n      const items = rows.map(rowToInfo)\n      if (!search) return items.slice(0, input?.limit ?? baseURL.replace(/\\/$/, \"\").replace(/\\/v1$/, \"\")\n  const ctrl = new AbortController()\n  const timer = setTimeout(() => ctrl.abort(), 400)\n  try {\n    const res = await fetch(`${root}/api/tags`, { signal: ctrl.signal })\n    if (!res.ok) return\n    const data = (await res.json()) as { models?: Array<{ name?: string }> }\n    const names = (data.models ?? []).map((m) => m.name).filter((n): n is string => Boolean(n))\n    if (preferred) {\n      const hit = names.find((n) => n === preferred || n.startsWith(`${preferred}:`))\n      if (hit) return { root, model: hit }\n    }\n    for (const p of OLLAMA_PREFERRED) {\n      const hit = names.find((n) => n === p || n.startsWith(`${p}:`))\n      if (hit) return { root, model: hit }\n    }\n    const embedish = names.find((n) => /embed/i.test(n))\n    if (embedish) return { root, model: embedish }\n  } catch {\n    return\n  } finally {\n    clearTimeout(timer)\n  }\n}\n\nfunction providerEmbedder(apiKey: string, model: string): ResolvedEmbedder {\n  const client = createOpenAI({ apiKey })\n  return {\n    kind: \"provider\",\n    modelId: `openai:${model}`,\n    label: `云端向量（openai/${model}）`,\n    embed: async (texts) => {\n      const result = await embedMany({\n        model: client.embedding(model),\n        values: texts,\n      })\n      return result.embeddings\n    },\n  }\n}\n\nfunction ollamaEmbedder(root: string, model: string): ResolvedEmbedder {\n  const client = createOpenAICompatible({\n    name: \"ollama\",\n    baseURL: `${root}/v1`,\n  })\n  return {\n    kind: \"ollama\",\n    modelId: `ollama:${model}`,\n    label: `本地 Ollama 向量（${model}）`,\n    embed: async (texts) => {\n      // openai-compatible embedding model accessor differs by SDK version\n      const embeddingModel =\n        \"embedding\" in client && typeof (client as any).embedding === \"function\"\n          ? (client as any).embedding(model)\n          : (client as any).textEmbeddingModel(model)\n      const result = await embedMany({\n        model: embeddingModel,\n        values: texts,\n      })\n      return result.embeddings\n    },\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Resolve the active embedder.\n * Safe to call frequently; results are cached briefly.\n */\nexport async function resolveEmbedder(cfg: EmbedConfig, opts?: { force?: boolean }): Promise<ResolvedEmbedder> {\n  const key = cacheKey(cfg)\n  const now = Date.now()\n  if (!opts?.force && cache && cache.key === key && now - cache.at < CACHE_TTL_MS) return cache.value\n\n  const mode = cfg.embedding_mode\n  let value: ResolvedEmbedder = localBackend()\n\n  if (mode === \"off\") value = offBackend()\n  else if (mode === \"local\") value = localBackend()\n  else if (mode === \"provider\") {\n    const apiKey = readOpenAIKey()\n    value = apiKey\n      ? providerEmbedder(apiKey, cfg.embedding_model)\n      : {\n          kind: \"local\",\n          modelId: \"local:ngram\",\n          label: \"本地语义（未配置云端 API Key，已回退）\",\n        }\n  } else if (mode === \"ollama\") {\n    const probed = await probeOllama(cfg.embedding_ollama_url, cfg.embedding_ollama_model)\n    value = probed\n      ? ollamaEmbedder(probed.root, cfg.embedding_ollama_model || probed.model)\n      : {\n          kind: \"local\",\n          modelId: \"local:ngram\",\n          label: \"本地语义（未检测到 Ollama 嵌入模型，已回退）\",\n        }\n  } else {\n    // auto: prefer cloud key already present, then ollama, else local\n    const apiKey = readOpenAIKey()\n    if (apiKey) {\n      value = providerEmbedder(apiKey, cfg.embedding_model)\n    } else {\n      const probed = await probeOllama(cfg.embedding_ollama_url, cfg.embedding_ollama_model)\n      value = probed\n        ? ollamaEmbedder(probed.root, cfg.embedding_ollama_model || probed.model)\n        : localBackend()\n    }\n  }\n\n  cache = { key, at: now, value }\n  return value\n}\n\n/** Best-effort single-text embed. Returns null for local/off or on failure. */\nexport async function embedText(cfg: EmbedConfig, text: string): Promise<{ vector: number[]; modelId: string } | null> {\n  const backend = await resolveEmbedder(cfg)\n  if (!backend.embed) return null\n  const trimmed = text.trim()\n  if (!trimmed) return null\n  try {\n    const [vector] = await backend.embed([trimmed])\n    if (!vector?.length) return null\n    return { vector, modelId: backend.modelId }\n  } catch {\n    return null\n  }\n}\n\nexport function clearEmbedderCache() {\n  cache = undefined\n}\n''', encoding=\"utf-8\")\nprint(\"embedder written\")\n'@ | python -",
  "workdir": "E:\\AImoney\\NovaWay-Matrix\\novaway-coder\\packages\\opencode"
}
```

{
  "command": "@'\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\n# User guide\nPath(r\"docs/memory-retrieval-guide.md\").write_text('''# 记忆智能召回使用指南\n\nNovaWay 的记忆检索默认**开箱即用**，不要求你安装任何本地模型。  \n有云端 API Key 或本机 Ollama 时，系统会自动升级到更强的向量召回。\n\n---\n\n## 1. 你现在就能用（零配置）\n\n只要打开了「启用持久记忆」，系统已经在用：\n\n1. 关键词匹配  \n2. SQLite FTS 全文检索  \n3. **本地语义**（字符 n-gram，不需要模型）\n\n### 怎么验证\n\n1. 打开 **设置 → 记忆与进化**\n2. 确认 **启用持久记忆** 为开\n3. 在对话中说：`请记住：我偏好简洁中文，先结论后细节`\n4. **新开一个会话**，再问：`我的回复偏好是什么？`\n5. 若能答对，说明基础记忆与本地召回已生效\n\n> 默认 `embedding_mode = auto`：没有外部模型时，自动使用本地语义，不会报错、不会卡住。\n\n---\n\n## 2. 想要更强的“近义召回”怎么做\n\n当用户说「喜欢简洁」而记忆里写的是「偏好精炼中文」时，稠密向量通常比纯关键词更稳。  \n有两种增强方式，**二选一即可**。\n\n### 方式 A：用你已经配置的云端模型（推荐，最省事）\n\n适合：你已经在 NovaWay 里配置过 OpenAI 等云端 Provider。\n\n1. 确保环境变量或 Provider 中已有可用的 OpenAI API Key  \n   - 常见：`OPENAI_API_KEY`\n2. 保持默认：\n\n```jsonc\n{\n  \"memory\": {\n    \"embedding_mode\": \"auto\"\n    // 或显式：\n    // \"embedding_mode\": \"provider\",\n    // \"embedding_provider\": \"openai\",\n    // \"embedding_model\": \"text-embedding-3-small\"\n  }\n}\n```\n\n3. 重启桌面端 / 后端一次  \n4. 之后新写入的记忆会自动生成稠密向量；召回时优先用向量相似度\n\n**说明：**\n\n- 不会额外要求你再买一套“记忆专用服务”\n- 没有 Key 时会自动退回本地语义，功能不中断\n\n### 方式 B：本机 Ollama 本地嵌入模型\n\n适合：希望数据留在本机、不想走云端。\n\n1. 安装并启动 [Ollama](https://ollama.com)\n2. 拉取一个嵌入模型，例如：\n\n```bash\nollama pull nomic-embed-text\n```\n\n也可使用：`mxbai-embed-large`、`bge-m3`、`all-minilm` 等带 embed 的模型。\n\n3. 确认服务可访问（默认 `http://localhost:11434`）\n4. 配置（可选，auto 通常会自动探测）：\n\n```jsonc\n{\n  \"memory\": {\n    \"embedding_mode\": \"auto\"\n    // 或强制本地：\n    // \"embedding_mode\": \"ollama\",\n    // \"embedding_ollama_url\": \"http://localhost:11434\",\n    // \"embedding_ollama_model\": \"nomic-embed-text\"\n  }\n}\n```\n\n5. 重启 NovaWay  \n6. 新写入记忆后，召回会优先使用本地稠密向量\n\n**没有装 Ollama / 没拉模型时：**  \n自动回退本地语义，不影响正常使用。\n\n---\n\n## 3. 模式怎么选\n\n| 模式 | 适用场景 | 是否需要模型 |\n|------|----------|--------------|\n| `auto`（默认） | 绝大多数用户 | 不需要；有则自动增强 |\n| `local` | 完全离线、内网管控 | 不需要 |\n| `provider` | 强制云端向量 | 需要云端 API Key |\n| `ollama` | 强制本机 Ollama | 需要本机 Ollama + 嵌入模型 |\n| `off` | 只要关键词/FTS | 不需要 |\n\n---\n\n## 4. 配置放哪里\n\n任选其一：\n\n### 项目级\n\n项目根目录 `novaway.json` / `novaway.jsonc`：\n\n```jsonc\n{\n  \"memory\": {\n    \"enabled\": true,\n    \"embedding_mode\": \"auto\"\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### 全局级\n\n全局配置目录中的 `novaway.json`（与你的 NovaWay 全局配置同目录）。\n\n### 设置页\n\n**设置 → 记忆与进化 → 智能召回**  \n可直接切换召回模式（见应用内说明）。\n\n---\n\n## 5. 日常使用怎么触发记忆\n\n### 写入\n\n- 显式：`请记住……` / `我的偏好是……`\n- 自动：开启智能归纳后，系统会从对话提炼长期信息\n\n### 召回\n\n- 正常聊天即可；系统会按需注入相关记忆摘要\n- 模型也可调用 `memory` 工具深挖\n\n### 建议自测句\n\n```text\n请记住：跨项目都用简体中文，先给方案再写代码。\n```\n\n新会话：\n\n```text\n我之前说过沟通和编码偏好吗？\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6. 常见问题\n\n### Q: 必须装本地模型吗？\n**不必须。** 默认本地语义即可用。\n\n### Q: 为什么我开了 auto，还是感觉没“变聪明”？\n1. 先确认记忆开关已开，并且「已写入记忆」里有条目  \n2. 新开会话再问（同会话上下文不等于长期记忆）  \n3. 若要用云端/Ollama 增强，确认 Key 或 Ollama 模型可用后**重启**  \n4. 稠密向量主要作用于**新写入**的记忆；旧记忆可重新说一次偏好触发更新\n\n### Q: 企业内网不能联网怎么办？\n```jsonc\n{ \"memory\": { \"embedding_mode\": \"local\" } }\n```\n或自建 Ollama 后使用 `ollama` 模式。\n\n### Q: 会不会偷偷调用外部 API？\n- `local` / 无 Key 的 `auto`：不会因记忆检索去调外部 embedding  \n- 仅在检测到可用云端 Key 且模式允许时，才会用云端 embedding\n\n---\n\n## 7. 一句话操作清单\n\n1. **什么都不配** → 直接用（本地语义）  \n2. **已有 OpenAI Key** → 保持 `auto`，重启即可增强  \n3. **想纯本地增强** → `ollama pull nomic-embed-text`，保持 `auto` 或设 `ollama`  \n4. **强制离线最稳** → `embedding_mode: \"local\"`\n''', encoding=\"utf-8\")\nprint(\"guide written\")\n'@ | python -",
  "workdir": "E:\\AImoney\\NovaWay-Matrix\\novaway-coder"
}
````

append using unicode escapes for reliability\n    extra = '''\n\n---\n\n## 8. \\u4e00\\u952e\\u542f\\u7528\\u672c\\u5730\\u5411\\u91cf\\uff08\\u63a8\\u8350\\uff09\n\n\\u5728 **\\u8bbe\\u7f6e \\u2192 \\u8bb0\\u5fc6\\u4e0e\\u8fdb\\u5316 \\u2192 \\u667a\\u80fd\\u53ec\\u56de** \\u70b9\\u51fb\\uff1a\n\n> **\\u4e00\\u952e\\u542f\\u7528\\u672c\\u5730\\u5411\\u91cf**\n\n\\u7cfb\\u7edf\\u4f1a\\u6309\\u987a\\u5e8f\\uff1a\n\n1. **\\u68c0\\u6d4b** \\u672c\\u673a\\u662f\\u5426\\u5df2\\u5b89\\u88c5 Ollama  \n2. **\\u672a\\u5b89\\u88c5** \\u5219\\u5c1d\\u8bd5\\u81ea\\u52a8\\u5b89\\u88c5\\uff08Windows: winget\\uff1bmacOS: brew\\uff1bLinux: \\u5b98\\u65b9\\u811a\\u672c\\uff09  \n3. **\\u5df2\\u5b89\\u88c5\\u4f46\\u672a\\u8fd0\\u884c** \\u5219\\u5c1d\\u8bd5\\u542f\\u52a8 `ollama serve`  \n4. **\\u7f3a\\u5d4c\\u5165\\u6a21\\u578b** \\u5219\\u6267\\u884c `ollama pull nomic-embed-text`  \n5. **\\u6210\\u529f\\u540e** \\u81ea\\u52a8\\u628a\\u53ec\\u56de\\u6a21\\u5f0f\\u5207\\u5230 `ollama`\n\n### \\u91cd\\u8981\\u8bf4\\u660e\n\n- **\\u4e0d\\u4f1a\\u5728\\u542f\\u52a8\\u65f6\\u9759\\u9ed8\\u5b89\\u88c5**\\uff0c\\u53ea\\u6709\\u4f60\\u70b9\\u6309\\u94ae\\u624d\\u4f1a\\u52a8  \n- \\u82e5\\u81ea\\u52a8\\u5b89\\u88c5\\u53d7\\u7cfb\\u7edf\\u6743\\u9650\\u9650\\u5236\\uff0c\\u4f1a\\u7ed9\\u51fa\\u624b\\u52a8\\u5b89\\u88c5\\u6307\\u5f15\\uff08\\u4e0b\\u8f7d\\u9875/\\u547d\\u4ee4\\uff09  \n- \\u4efb\\u4f55\\u4e00\\u6b65\\u5931\\u8d25\\u90fd\\u4e0d\\u4f1a\\u5f71\\u54cd\\u57fa\\u7840\\u8bb0\\u5fc6\\uff1b\\u7cfb\\u7edf\\u4ecd\\u53ef\\u7528\\u672c\\u5730 n-gram \\u8bed\\u4e49\n\n'''\n    guide.write_text(g + extra, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    print(\"guide updated\")\nelse:\n    print(\"guide already has one-click section or chinese check\")\n\nprint(\"tests + exports ready\")\n'@ | python -",

}

  "command": "@'\nfrom pathlib import Path\n# map status message in UI to Chinese for common phases - optional polish in settings\np = Path(r\"packages/app/src/components/settings-memory.tsx\")\nt = p.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n# Add a small helper display for status\nif \"formatEmbeddingStatus\" not in t:\n    t = t.replace(\n        'const embeddingMode = () => memoryConfig().embedding_mode ?? \"auto\"',\n        '''const embeddingMode = () => memoryConfig().embedding_mode ?? \"auto\"\n  const formatEmbeddingStatus = () => {\n    const data = embeddingStatus.data as\n      | {\n          ready?: boolean\n          cliInstalled?: boolean\n          daemonRunning?: boolean\n          hasEmbedModel?: boolean\n          selectedModel?: string\n          activeBackendLabel?: string\n          message?: string\n          hint?: string\n        }\n      | undefined\n    if (embeddingStatus.isLoading) return \"正在检测本机 Ollama…\"\n    if (!data) return \"尚未检测\"\n    if (data.ready) return `本地向量已就绪${data.selectedModel ? `（${data.selectedModel}）` : \"\"}`\n    if (!data.cliInstalled) return \"未检测到 Ollama（可一键尝试安装）\"\n    if (!data.daemonRunning) return \"已安装 Ollama，但服务未运行\"\n    if (!data.hasEmbedModel) return \"Ollama 已运行，仍需拉取嵌入模型\"\n    return data.message || \"状态未知\"\n  }\n'''\n    )\n    t = t.replace(\n        '{embeddingStatus.data?.message ||\\n                  (embeddingStatus.isLoading ? \"检测中…\" : \"尚未检测\")}',\n        \"{formatEmbeddingStatus()}\",\n    )\n    p.write_text(t, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    print(\"UI status formatter added\")\nelse:\n    print(\"formatter exists\")\n'@ | python -",
